## 5. H36M-Mirror / SemGCN source

- `h36m_mirror/SemGCN/h36m.zip`: 248.0 MB — SemGCN zip; needs conversion to multiview npz

## 6. Missing conversions / blockers

- `data/h36m_mirror/SemGCN/h36m.zip` is present but not converted to the multiview `.npz` format used by v4. A conversion script is needed for subjects S1/S5/S6/S7/S8 (train) and S9/S11 (val/test).
- AIST++ data are still in `.pkl`/`.json` split form. A conversion to the unified `points_2d/confidences/joints_3d/camera_*` `.npz` format is required before v4 training.

## 7. Proposed v4 large-scale training manifest

| Split | Dataset | Subjects / Sequences | Cameras | Frames (est.) | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| train | MPI-INF-3DHP | S1–S8 all sequences | 4 / 14 | ~1.2M | Ready (v14/v4 npz) |
| train | H36M-HF | S1, S5, S6, S7, S8 | 4 | ~250k | Partial (only S1/S9/S11 present) |
| train | H36M-Mirror | S1, S5–S8 | 4 | ~250k | Needs conversion |
| train | WebBridge-H36M | All available sequences | 4 | ~150k | Ready |
| train | AIST++ | All motion sequences (after conversion) | ~10 | ~1.5M | Needs conversion |
| val | H36M-HF | S9 | 4 | ~30k | Partial (only S9 act 02 present) |
| test | H36M-HF | S11 | 4 | ~30k | Partial (only S11 act 02 present) |
| test | MPI-INF-3DHP | Test set TS1–TS6 | 14 | ~30k | Ready (17-joint npz) |
| test | 3DPW | validation/test sequences | 1 actual / 4 pseudo | ~50k | Ready (actual + pseudo npz) |
| test | Shelf/Campus | Shelf/Campus sequences | 5 / 3 | ~5k | Ready |

## 8. Recommendations

1. **Convert H36M-Mirror zip** to the same multiview `.npz` schema as `data/h36m_hf/`. This is the biggest blocker for a full S1/S5–S8 train split.
2. **Convert AIST++** `.pkl`/`.json` split into unified `.npz` bundles. AIST++ provides the largest dance-motion diversity once converted.
3. **Normalize MPI-INF-3DHP test-set `.npz`** — TS1–TS6 `.npz` already exists (14 views, 17 joints), but the skeleton convention differs from the 28-joint MPI training data; align joints before v4 variable-view evaluation.
4. **Normalize skeleton convention** across MPI (28 joints), H36M (17 joints), 3DPW (24 joints), and AIST++ (24/17 joints) before v4 multi-dataset training.
5. **Generate smoke manifests** in `configs/splits/` pointing only to files verified above, so the v4 trainer can fail fast on missing conversions.
